For fallback, host.\n\nKernel formula:\n```\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(\n    const int* __restrict__ type,\n    const double* __restrict__ strike,\n    const double* __restrict__ spot,\n    const double* __restrict__ q,\n    const double* __restrict__ r,\n    const double* __restrict__ t,\n    const double* __restrict__ vol,\n    double* __restrict__ out,\n    size_t n)\n{\n    size_t idx = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    size_t stride = (size_t)gridDim.x * blockDim.x;\n    for (; idx < n; idx += stride) {\n        double S=spot[idx], K=strike[idx], T=t[idx], sigma=vol[idx];\n        if (T<=0 || sigma<=0) { out[idx]=0; continue; }\n        double sqrtT = sqrt(T);\n        double d1 = (log(S/K) + (r[idx]-q[idx]+0.5*sigma*sigma)*T)/(sigma*sqrtT);\n        double d2 = d1 - sigma*sqrtT;\n        double Nd1 = cumulativeNormalDevice(d1);\n        double Nd2 = cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);\n        double discount=exp(-r[idx]*T);\n        double div=exp(-q[idx]*T);\n        double price;\n        if (type[idx]==CALL)\n            price = S*div*Nd1 - K*discount*Nd2;\n        else\n            price = K*discount*(1.0-Nd2) - S*div*(1.0-Nd1);\n        out[idx]=price;\n    }\n}\n```\nWe can precompute `r-q+0.5 sigma^2`.
